# The native asset bundle (src/sys/native_assets.c): boot.state + disc.vf3,
# built from the user's own disc dump.  With it `vf3sdl` starts with no .gdi,
# track files, BIOS ROM or emulator savestate.  The target is empty when the
# disc data is not present, so a source-only checkout still builds.
option(VF3_NATIVE_ASSETS "Pack the native asset bundle when the disc data is present" ON)
add_custom_target(vf3assets)
if (VF3_NATIVE_ASSETS)
    find_package(Python3 COMPONENTS Interpreter QUIET)
    set(VF3_ASSET_IMAGE "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/extract/gamedata/1ST_READ.BIN")
    set(VF3_ASSET_GDI   "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/rom/vf3.gdi")
    set(VF3_ASSET_TRACK "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/rom/track03.bin")
    set(VF3_ASSET_DIR   "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vf3_assets")
    if (Python3_Interpreter_FOUND AND EXISTS "${VF3_ASSET_IMAGE}"
        AND EXISTS "${VF3_ASSET_GDI}" AND EXISTS "${VF3_ASSET_TRACK}")
        add_custom_command(
            OUTPUT "${VF3_ASSET_DIR}/manifest.json"
            COMMAND "${Python3_EXECUTABLE}" "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/tools/make_native_assets.py"
                    --gdi "${VF3_ASSET_GDI}" --image "${VF3_ASSET_IMAGE}"
                    --out "${VF3_ASSET_DIR}"
            DEPENDS "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/tools/make_native_assets.py"
                    "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/tools/make_boot_state.py"
                    "${VF3_ASSET_IMAGE}" "${VF3_ASSET_GDI}" "${VF3_ASSET_TRACK}"
            COMMENT "Packing the native asset bundle (boot.state + disc.vf3)"
            VERBATIM)
        add_custom_target(vf3assets-build DEPENDS "${VF3_ASSET_DIR}/manifest.json")
        add_dependencies(vf3assets vf3assets-build)
    else()
        message(STATUS "VF3 native assets: disc data not found; the bundle is not built")
    endif()
endif()

# The SDL3 host (docs/re/plan_sdl3_port.md, phase P0).  Off by default: the
# port itself does not need SDL3, only the host that presents its frames.
option(VF3_SDL3 "Build the SDL3 host (vf3sdl)" OFF)
if (VF3_SDL3)
    find_package(SDL3 CONFIG QUIET)
    if (NOT SDL3_FOUND)
        # No CMake package config: find the headers and the import library
        # directly (MSYS2's ucrt64 SDL3, or a plain install).
        find_path(SDL3_INCLUDE_DIR NAMES SDL3/SDL.h
                  HINTS "$ENV{MSYS2_ROOT}/ucrt64/include" "C:/msys64/ucrt64/include")
        find_library(SDL3_LIBRARY NAMES SDL3 SDL3.dll
                     HINTS "$ENV{MSYS2_ROOT}/ucrt64/lib" "C:/msys64/ucrt64/lib")
        if (NOT SDL3_INCLUDE_DIR OR NOT SDL3_LIBRARY)
            message(FATAL_ERROR
                    "SDL3 not found. Install it (MSYS2: pacman -S mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-sdl3) "
                    "or point SDL3_INCLUDE_DIR and SDL3_LIBRARY at it.")
        endif()
        add_library(SDL3::SDL3 UNKNOWN IMPORTED)
        set_target_properties(SDL3::SDL3 PROPERTIES
                              IMPORTED_LOCATION "${SDL3_LIBRARY}"
                              INTERFACE_INCLUDE_DIRECTORIES "${SDL3_INCLUDE_DIR}")
    endif()
    add_executable(vf3sdl src/host/sdl3_host.c src/host/sdl3_input.c src/render/pvr2.c src/render/ta.c src/sys/frame_events.c)
    target_link_libraries(vf3sdl PRIVATE vf3core SDL3::SDL3)
    add_dependencies(vf3sdl vf3assets)

    # The host's keyboard: the SDL events it turns into the pad's wire format
    # (the wire's own test, tests/maple_input_replay.c, drives the Maple model).
    add_executable(vf3sdlinput tests/sdl3_input_replay.c src/host/sdl3_input.c)
    target_link_libraries(vf3sdlinput PRIVATE vf3core SDL3::SDL3)
endif()

# Shared oracle .cases runner: add_port_test(<target> <test-source>)
function(add_port_test tname src)
  add_executable(${tname} ${src})
  target_link_libraries(${tname} PRIVATE vf3core)
  target_include_directories(${tname} PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests)
endfunction()

add_port_test(vf3scalemap tests/scalemap_replay.c)
# the REIOS GD-ROM service: the BIOS vector must be answered by the drive that
# is attached to the run, not by the idle model (docs/re/gd_service.md)

# the host's pad must reach the guest in the Maple wire's format (the button
# bits and the analog axes)

# way the hardware does (docs/re/aica_audio_model.md)

# frames (docs/re/aica_audio_model.md)
